【黃河實現連續27年不斷流】記者從水利部瞭解到，位於黃河最下游的國家水文站——利津水文站12日測得實時流量2030立方米每秒。黃河已實現自1999年以來連續27年不斷流。水利部相關負責人介紹，水利部黃河水利委員會於1999年啟動黃河水量統一排程。27年來，黃河水量排程從著眼“確保不斷流”的基礎目標，逐步升級為多目標統籌的精細排程，成為守護母親河健康生命的核心支撐。（新華社）

US Mortgage Applications Rebound — The volume of mortgage applications in the US rose by 3.6% on the first week of August, the largest increase in two months, and rebounding from two straight periods of decline, according to data compiled by the Mortgage Bankers Association. The result was aligned with the slight respite for benchmark mortgage rates, tracking the momentary recovery for long-term Treasury bonds from their slide multi-decade highs. Applications to refinance a mortgage, which are more sensitive to short-term changes in interest rates, rose by 5%. In turn, applications for a mortgage to purchase a home rose by 3%.

### 2026-08-12 19:11:14  #公司

【鋰雲母提鋰多項技術難題攻克 碳酸鋰供需偏緊】鋰雲母提鋰多項技術難題攻克，為國內鋰資源供給注入強心劑。科技日報報道，近日，中鋁國際長沙有色冶金設計研究院有限公司牽頭完成的“鋰雲母高效提鋰技術與裝備研發及應用”專案，成功攻克了我國鋰雲母提鋰長期面臨的裝置腐蝕、迴轉窯結圈、回收率低等重大技術難題，實現大規模工業化生產，標誌著我國複雜低品位鋰雲母資源高值化利用邁出關鍵一步。供給端來看，近期中礦資源、天華新能、九嶺鋰業旗下碳酸鋰產線陸續公佈了8月至9月的停產檢修安排。近期的集中檢修將在供給端造成階段性減量。供給短期收縮的同時，碳酸鋰下游消費持續旺盛，儲能電池貢獻需求的第二增長引擎。今年上半年，碳酸鋰價格顯著回升。據Wind資料，電池級碳酸鋰上半年均價為16.34萬元/噸，2025年上半年均價為7.04萬元/噸，價格實現翻倍式增長。鋰礦股業績隨之提振，統計顯示，目前已有22只鋰礦股釋出半年度業績相關公告。5只鋰礦股上半年實現扭虧為盈，12股實現歸母淨利潤同比增長，報喜比例超七成。從資金面上來看，8月以來，10只鋰礦股獲融資淨買入，西部礦業、科達製造、中礦資源淨買入金額居前，分別達到3.63億元、0.76億元、0.5億元。

### 2026-08-12 19:10:50  #公司

【浙江寧波：分散式光伏裝機破千萬千瓦】記者從國網寧波供電公司獲悉，8月12日，隨著寧波前灣新區寧波科學中學1038千瓦分散式光伏專案併網投運，浙江省寧波市分散式光伏裝機規模突破千萬千瓦。據介紹，寧波分散式光伏主要佈局在工業園區、公共建築和居民屋頂等場景，其中工業園區裝機佔比約84%。按年等效滿發小時數1000小時測算，寧波市分散式光伏年發電量可達100億千瓦時，每年可減少二氧化碳排放約500萬噸。面對分散式光伏大規模接入帶來的出力波動、區域性消納和配網執行等挑戰，國網寧波供電公司透過電網模擬平臺、分散式光伏執行監測應用、市級虛擬電廠等手段，提升新能源預測、排程和消納能力。同時，面向工業園區推出“電能碳”一體化服務和“陽光掌櫃”數字化結算平臺，提升園區綜合用能服務效率，促進屋頂綠電就地消納。（新華社）

【美媒：伊朗把美國拖入不對稱戰爭】自2月底以來，伊朗戰事已從美國起初預計的“四周”延宕逾5個月；美方從大規模猛烈空襲，到打擊勢頭大幅減弱並尋求談判。美國媒體說，在這場戰事中，伊朗不斷汲取經驗，針對美方短板打不對稱戰爭，以弱制強。 《紐約時報》11日在報道中說，隨著時間推移，這場戰事顯現兩個重要趨勢。第一，伊朗的戰術運用越來越嫻熟，不僅學會如何突破美方防空系統，而且拉上葉門胡塞武裝和伊拉克的民兵組織擴大戰線。其次，美軍防空系統攔截彈消耗迅速，攔截能力越來越捉襟見肘。 訊息人士披露，戰事開始以來，美軍已消耗1500多枚“愛國者”防空系統的攔截彈，是2025年美國全年產量的兩倍多，目前僅剩不到1700枚。 美方一再聲稱已經重創伊朗軍事實力。國防部長赫格塞思4月初表示，伊朗導彈及發射裝置“幾乎徹底失去作用”。然而，報道稱，美國轟炸後，伊朗挖出被埋在地下隧道里的導彈及發射裝置，繼續用於封鎖霍爾木茲海峽和打擊中東地區美軍目標。（新華社）

US Mortgage Rates Fall Last Week: MBA — The average 30-year fixed mortgage rate for loans of up to $806,500 in the US edged down 4bps to 6.77% in the week ended August 7, 2026, from 6.81% in the previous week, which marked the highest level in a year, according to the Mortgage Bankers Association. The decline tracked lower Treasury yields after a weaker-than-expected jobs report reduced expectations for an imminent Fed rate hike. Mortgage applications increased 3.6%, marking the first rise in three weeks, with applications to purchase a home up 2.5% and those to refinance a home loan rising 5%.

【農耕記外賣店員工邊抽菸邊切青椒 門店回應：培訓不到位，已辭退涉事員工】湘菜連鎖品牌“農耕記”北京一家外賣門店陷入食品安全爭議。有消費者反映，該店後廚員工存在邊抽菸邊處理食材的不規範行為。據消費者發帖稱，其在路過農耕記北京望京南外賣店時，看到一名後廚員工一邊抽菸，一邊處理青椒。針對此事，門店負責人承認情況屬實，並表示事件發生於今日上午，下午門店接到通知，目前已開出涉事員工，並對相關食材進行了廢棄處理。該工作人員解釋稱，涉事員工為新員工，入職僅一個月左右，門店對員工培訓不到位，後續也會加強管理。新浪財經注意到，該涉事門店為美團“明廚亮灶”商家。前述門店負責人表示，“明廚亮灶”要求攝像頭對準炒菜加工區域，摘辣椒的環節不在其覆蓋範圍內，不過門店內也有安裝其他攝像頭。該工作人員還透露，該事件已引起當地市場監管部門關注，門店將接受調查，可能面臨相應罰款。

【央行：加強對擴大內需、科技創新、中小微企業等重點領域的金融支援】央行釋出2026年第二季度中國貨幣政策執行報告，下階段，充分發揮貨幣信貸政策導向作用。用好各類結構性貨幣政策工具，不斷完善工具設計和管理，紮實做好金融“五篇大文章”，加強對擴大內需、科技創新、中小微企業等重點領域的金融支援。發揮科技創新和技術改造再貸款、債券市場“科技板”作用，強化科技金融資料共享，加快構建高水平的區域科技金融生態，推動加強科技創新全鏈條全生命週期金融服務。最佳化綠色金融標準，持續健全碳核算制度體系，豐富綠色金融產品譜系，完善金融支援零碳園區建設政策舉措。健全民營中小企業增信制度，進一步開展金融服務能力提升工程，助力民營和小微企業融資發展。創新鄉村振興投融資機制，推進鄉村全面振興。實施好創業擔保貸款、國家助學貸款政策，支援重點群體創業就業和就學。推動金融機構加大養老產業信貸支援，支援金融機構研發符合養老需求、具有長期限特徵和一定收益率的養老金融產品，提升金融適老化水平。強化金融支援提振和擴大消費作用，推動服務業擴能提質。

### 2026-08-12 18:40:18  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Inflation Rate #Importance 2

India Inflation Rises to 20-Month High — The headline inflation rate in India rose to 4.45% in July of 2026 from 4.38% in the previous month, the highest since December of 2024, and aligned with market expectations of 4.5%. The increase reflected fresh inflationary pressures from the surge in energy prices since the outbreak of war in the Middle East, magnified by the consequent pressure on the rupee. Still, the rate remained comfortably within the RBI's tolerance range of 2 percentage points away from 4%. Inflation was elevated for food and beverages (5.52%) and transportation (4.43%), both impacted by the war and adverse weather conditions for key crops. In turn, inflation was more muted for housing and utilities (2.16%) and recreation, sport, and culture (1.6%). From the previous month, the Indian CPI rose by 0.88%.

【PBOC to build comprehensive macroprudential framework, expand policy toolkit】People's Bank of China released its Q2 2026 monetary policy implementation report, flagging a push to build a comprehensive macroprudential management system and enrich the macroprudential policy toolkit. From macro, countercyclical and contagion‑prevention perspectives it will strengthen monitoring, assessment and early‑warning of systemic financial risks and broaden macroprudential coverage. The central bank said it will expand macroprudential and financial‑stability functions, innovate financial instruments to preserve market stability, and deepen macroprudential oversight of systemically important financial institutions, progressively extending additional supervisory measures into the non‑bank sector. For systemically important banks it will reinforce add‑on supervision, require selected banks to improve recovery and resolution plans, explore forward‑looking risk‑management guidance, and bolster cross‑border crisis‑management group arrangements and supervisory information‑sharing for global systemically important banks.

### 2026-08-12 18:34:17  #央行 #其他

【央行：金融支援實體經濟已由更加註重規模擴張轉向更加註重質量和效率，融資渠道更加豐富，資源配置更加最佳化，金融適配性不斷增強】央行釋出2026年第二季度中國貨幣政策執行報告。金融對實體經濟的支援不僅是宏觀金融總量的持續擴張，更多體現為質效的不斷提升。一方面，隨著適度寬鬆的貨幣政策有力實施，金融機構服務實體經濟的能力不斷增強，實體企業融資感受不斷提升、融資成本持續下行。另一方面，融資結構持續最佳化。社融、M2 存量已分別超過 460 萬億元、350 萬億元，貸款規模也超過 280 萬億元，提高存量資金使用效率和最佳化新增融資投向一樣，都對實體經濟發揮了積極作用。房地產和地方政府融資平臺貸款到期償還規模較大，新發放貸款更多投向代表經濟新動能的高效領域，此消彼長之間，貸款增量沒有原來多，但信貸結構持續最佳化，普惠小微貸款、科技貸款、綠色貸款增速已連續多年高於各項貸款增速。特別是金融資源向科技創新、先進製造等重點領域加速集聚，深刻賦能新一輪科技革命和產業變革的程序，A 股上市的“專精特新”企業達 2000 多家，債券市場“科技板”落地一年多來，科技創新債券已累計發行超過 2.8 萬億元。總的看，金融支援實體經濟已由更加註重規模擴張轉向更加註重質量和效率，融資渠道更加豐富，資源配置更加最佳化，金融適配性不斷增強。在此背景下，觀察金融支援實體經濟的力度和質效不宜再侷限於貸款的單一渠道、規模與增速的單一視角，而是需要更加多維全面，科學分析金融體系為高質量發展提供的全方位支援。

【水利部滾動部署應對臺風“白海豚”殘餘環流影響】水利部12日舉行專題會商，滾動分析今年第13號颱風“白海豚”殘餘環流影響態勢，深入研判突出風險，進一步安排部署颱風暴雨洪水防禦工作。 記者瞭解到，颱風“白海豚”雖已停止編號，但其殘餘環流與冷空氣結合，仍將造成大範圍持續性強降雨，持續影響太湖流域、長江中下游、淮河流域西部南部、黃河中游小花區間、海河流域南部、遼河流域東北部。據預報，8月12日至16日，太湖及周邊河網地區河流將持續維持超警；長江下游支流滁河、巢湖，漢江中下游幹流，淮河上中游幹流及支流淠河、洪汝河、沙潁河、渦河，海河流域衛河等主要河湖將出現明顯漲水過程，其中，沙潁河、洪汝河、滁河將發生編號洪水，淮河中游幹流、漢江中下游幹流接近警戒水位；暴雨區內部分中小河流可能發生超警以上洪水，山洪災害風險高。 面對汛情，水利部相關負責人要求做好七個方面重點工作。一要強化監測預報預警，二要突出抓好山洪災害防禦，三要強化流域水工程統一聯合排程，四要確保水庫安全度汛，五要強化堤防巡查防守，六要統籌做好城市外洪內澇防禦，七要確保南水北調中線工程安全。（新華社）

### 2026-08-12 18:28:03  #央行 #宏觀 #市場

【2026年第二季度中國貨幣政策執行報告】一是保持銀行體系流動性充裕，金融總量合理增長。綜合運用逆回購、中期借貸便利、國債買賣操作等多種貨幣政策工具，保持流動性充裕。完善短端利率調控機制，在公開市場操作中增加隔夜逆回購操作品種，並將臨時正、逆回購操作區間寬度由70個基點收窄至50個基點，增強貨幣市場利率調控的精準性、有效性。創設境外央行回購工具，便利境外央行類機構的人民幣流動性管理和人民幣債券資產配置。引導金融機構信貸均衡投放，有力支援實體經濟有效信貸需求。二是推動社會綜合融資成本低位執行。下調結構性貨幣政策工具利率0.25個百分點，持續推進明示企業貸款綜合融資成本工作，降低融資中間費用。更好發揮市場利率定價自律機制作用，維護市場公平競爭秩序，提升銀行自主理性定價能力。三是激勵引導金融機構加大對重點領域的支援力度。出臺並落實好一系列結構性貨幣政策措施，擴大工具規模和支援範圍，完善政策要素，合併使用支農支小再貸款與再貼現額度，增加支農支小再貸款以及科技創新和技術改造再貸款額度，單設1萬億元民營企業再貸款，合併設立科技創新與民營企業債券風險分擔工具，進一步助力經濟結構轉型最佳化。積極穩妥實施一次性信用修復政策，支援符合條件的逾期人群高效便捷重塑信用。四是保持匯率基本穩定。堅持市場供求在匯率形成中起決定性作用，發揮好匯率對宏觀經濟、國際收支的調節功能，綜合施策，保持人民幣匯率在合理均衡水平上的基本穩定。鼓勵金融機構提升匯率避險服務水平。在上海自貿區開展離岸人民幣外匯交易，促進在岸和離岸人民幣市場融合。五是穩妥有序推動重點領域風險處置。穩步推進重點機構和重點區域金融風險處置。銀行間市場資料包告庫正式掛牌，提升對金融市場的穿透監測能力。

【Tianwan Unit 7 begins first fuel loading】China National Nuclear Corporation (CNNC) said initial fuel loading has begun at Tianwan nuclear plant Unit 7, marking the first domestic fuel loading of a VVER-1200 reactor. The 1.265 GW unit has a 60-year design life, is expected to generate nearly 10 bln kWh annually and is slated for commercial operation in 2026. When all eight Tianwan units are online, site capacity will exceed 9 GW, annual output will top 70 bln kWh and CO2 emissions will be cut by about 57.4 mln tonnes per year.

### 2026-08-12 18:19:28  #Trading Economics #Economy #Serbia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Serbia Inflation Slows to Lowest Since 2021 — The inflation rate in Serbia eased to 1.9% year-on-year in July 2026 from 2.7% in the previous month. The latest figure marked the weakest growth since March 2021, as prices grew at a softer pace for clothing and footwear (1.2% vs 1.4% in June), furnishings and household equipment (0.6% vs 0.8%), transportation (7.1% vs 7.7%), education (1.2% vs 1.8%), restaurants and hotels (6.1% vs 6.2%), insurance and financial services (3.9% vs 4.1%), and miscellaneous goods and services (0.7% vs 2.5%). At the same time, costs declined further for food and non-alcoholic beverages (-6.1% vs -3.7%). In contrast, prices increased for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (6.5% vs 5.2%), housing and utilities (9.7% vs 9.5%), recreation and culture (10.0% vs 9.7%), information and communication (1.9% vs 1.6%), and health (7.9% vs 7.6%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices fell by 0.2%, following a 0.2% rise in the preceding period.

### 2026-08-12 18:17:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #Portugal #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Portugal Inflation Rate Eases Further in July — Portugal’s annual inflation rate edged down to 3.0% in July 2026, easing for a third consecutive month and confirming the preliminary estimate. The slowdown was driven mainly by softer energy inflation, which fell to 8.7% from 9.1%. Goods inflation eased to 2.2% from 2.4%, reflecting slower food price growth (2.2% vs 3%), while services inflation declined to 4% from 4.2%, led by lower inflation for housing and utilities (3.2% vs 3.6%), transport (4.9% vs 5.5%), and restaurants and hotels (6% vs 6.2%). Inflation stood at 1.8% for furnishings and 4.4% for education. On a monthly basis, consumer prices fell 0.5% after rising 0.1% in June. Meanwhile, harmonised inflation held at 3.1%, near a two-year high and above the ECB’s 2% target.

【美中貿易全國委員會報告：缺乏精準度的出口管制正削弱美企對華競爭力】8月11日，美中貿易全國委員會（USCBC）釋出一份最新行業調查。調查結果表明，審批遲滯的美國出口許可制度正削弱美國產業對華競爭力。USCBC在7月開展了一項快速調查，以瞭解企業在特朗普第二任期內應對美國出口許可制度的實際情況。31家受訪企業主要來自科技、工業和製造業、能源以及醫療保健等行業。根據報告資料，66%的受訪企業表示，部分許可證申請已經等待至少3個月，超過了美國商務部依法應在90天內處理申請的期限；31%的企業表示，部分許可證申請已經等待1至2年。其中56%的企業還表示，在本屆政府之前，類似出口許可證的審批通常只需要1至3個月。（澎湃）

【Ukraine urges EU to target minerals feeding Russian arms industry】A Kyiv-based think tank and Ukraine's Economic Security Council urged the EU to target imports of critical minerals that sustain Russia's weapons production. Their report says Russia imported more than $1.1bn of strategic minerals for military-related uses over one year, including alumina, tantalum and molybdenum, and identifies other required inputs such as beryllium, tungsten, manganese, niobium, rhenium and titanium. Many of these commodities flowed into the supply chains of dozens of Russian firms that produce missiles, aircraft, armored vehicles, munitions, electronics and engines. The EU has not imposed export restrictions; proposed measures require unanimity among 27 member states and face pressure from some governments seeking exemptions or weaker language, complicating adoption.

### 2026-08-12 17:35:47  #公司 #市場 #觀點

【英偉達聯手六大巨頭5000億美元押注AI，但資本市場不買賬】英偉達在當地時間8月10日宣佈與阿波羅全球管理公司、黑石集團、貝萊德、博楓、高盛和KKR簽署諒解備忘錄，計劃建立算力融資平臺，目標是在長期內部署超過5000億美元的第三方資本，用於AI基礎設施建設。然而，這則重磅訊息並未給市場帶來驚喜，反而更像是驚嚇。訊息公佈後，英偉達股價當日盤中一度跌超3%，最終收跌2.86%；次日（當地時間8月11日），英偉達股價收盤小幅下跌0.02%，基本企穩。這或與英偉達對GPU資產屬性的“重新定義”有關。傳統觀點認為GPU屬於快速貶值的硬體，而英偉達試圖重新定義GPU的資產屬性。彭博社8月11日報道指出，英偉達已與AI生態系統內的企業簽署了價值數千億美元的交易，引發部分投資者擔憂這類協議具有迴圈交易性質，英偉達可能借此推高整個行業的需求和估值。自5月下旬至今，英偉達債務的違約保險成本近乎翻倍。這反映出市場對英偉達在鉅額融資模式下潛在信用風險的關注度正在上升。（中新經緯）

### 2026-08-12 17:34:13  #Trading Economics #Economy #Namibia #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Namibia Leaves Key Policy Rate Steady at 6.75% — The Bank of Namibia kept its benchmark repo rate unchanged at 6.75% during its August 2026 meeting, following a 25 bps rate hike in June. Policymakers said the policy stance was appropriate to support international reserves and maintain the one-to-one peg between the Namibian dollar and the South African rand. The Committee noted domestic inflationary pressures have remained elevated since the preceding meeting. Annual headline inflation rose to 4.4% in June 2026 from 4.1% in May, primarily driven by higher transport prices. Inflation is forecast to rise to 4.0% in 2026 from 3.5% in 2025, before easing to 3.9% in 2027, with the latter forecast revised up by 0.3 percentage point amid prolonged Middle East tensions.

【深圳市居民醫保參保繳費模式調整 ，9月1日起實施】近日，經深圳市政府同意，深圳市醫療保障局、國家稅務總局深圳市稅務局聯合印發《關於調整我市居民基本醫療保險參保繳費有關事項的通知》（以下簡稱《通知》），按照國家及廣東省的檔案精神最佳化完善深圳市居民醫保繳費模式，自9月1日起實施。 《通知》主要明確了“三個不變、兩個調整”，其中“三個不變”是指居民醫保參保範圍不變、居民醫保繳費費率不變以及居民醫保報銷比例不變，同時為落實國家、廣東省相關檔案要求進行了“兩個調整”，一是調整居民醫保參保時間，明確“集中參保期”為每年9月至12月；二是為了鼓勵持續參保，落實“待遇等待期”機制。 深圳市醫療保障局相關負責人介紹，新政策實施後，深圳居民醫保的繳費模式及待遇享受時間與全國其他地區保持一致，居民醫保參保人因工作、生活跨地區流動時，醫保接續會更加順暢，預計將有效解決之前因參保週期不同而產生的醫保“斷檔”問題。需要著重提醒的是，本次調整隻適用於深圳居民醫保參保人，對職工及退休人群沒有影響。 調整集中參保期，醫保銜接更順暢。深圳居民醫保集中參保期為每年9月至12月。參保人應當在集中參保期內辦理次年度居民醫保參保登記，並一次性繳納相應的居民醫保費。在集中參保期內參保繳費且連續參保的居民醫保參保人，自次年1月1日至12月31日享受待遇。深圳居民醫保參保繳費週期與其他地市保持一致，更有利於參保人跨地區醫保銜接。 實施待遇等待期機制，鼓勵連續參保。為了增強對長期按時繳費的參保人的公平性，防止“健康時不參保、生病才突擊繳費”的行為損害醫保基金“互助共濟”的根基，根據國家、廣東省有關檔案要求，《通知》對未在居民醫保集中參保期內參保或未連續參保的人員設定待遇等待期。斷保時間越長，待遇等待期相應延長，等待期內發生的醫療費用醫保統籌基金不予報銷，需自行承擔，待遇等待期具體規則按照國家、廣東省規定執行。同時，為做好銜接保障，新生兒出生當年或次年參加居民醫保並繳費的、收入型醫療救助物件等特殊群體不設待遇等待期。需要注意的是，深圳2027年度居民醫保集中參保期為2026年9月至12月，這個時間尤為重要，若未在該時間內參保繳費的，將會影響參保人2027年正常享受居民醫保待遇。 明確過渡期措施，確保待遇不中斷。

The official construction PMI fell to a record low of 47 in July, and construction starts sank by 23.4% in June annually. ### 2026-08-12 17:13:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #Montenegro #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Montenegro Annual Inflation Accelerates in July — Montenegro’s annual inflation rate rose to 3.8% in July 2026 from 3.6% in June, marking the fastest pace in recent months. Price growth accelerated for food (2.2% vs 2.1%), clothing and footwear (2.3% vs 0.9%), restaurants and hotels (5.4% vs 4.4%), information and communication (1.1% vs 0.7%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (10.4% vs 5.6%), furnishings (3.8% vs 3.5%) and health (4.3% vs 3.5%). In contrast, inflation eased for housing and utilities (2.1% vs 2.8%), transport (10.6% vs 12.8%) and recreation and culture (1.4% vs 1.8%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.8%, accelerating from 0.4% in June. Meanwhile, the harmonised inflation rate increased to 3.9% from 3.6%.

### 2026-08-12 17:10:44  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Rubber Holds in Tight Range — Rubber futures traded around 219 US cents per kilogram in mid-August, remaining range-bound since early July amid a lack of clear market direction. Higher oil prices provided some support to natural rubber by reducing the competitiveness of crude-based synthetic rubber, as concerns over the reopening of the Strait of Hormuz underpinned crude prices. However, persistent weakness in China’s domestic auto market, the world’s top rubber consumer, continued to cap gains amid concerns over tyre demand and rubber consumption. Domestic car sales fell 21.1% year-on-year in July to 1.47 million vehicles, marking a tenth consecutive monthly decline, although the pace of contraction eased. Meanwhile, vehicle exports surged 88.2% as automakers increasingly looked to overseas markets to offset intense competition at home.

### 2026-08-12 17:03:26  #公司 #國際

【華爾街月付10萬搶先幾毫秒看帖 特朗普帖子搶先看服務挨批】為搶先看到美國總統特朗普的社交媒體動態，華爾街高頻交易機構正不惜支付鉅款。 在本週舉行的首次財報電話會議上，特朗普媒體科技集團（TMTG）代理執行長麥格恩（Kevin McGurn）透露，已有10多家高頻交易機構為主的客戶付費訂閱了“真實社交”（Truth Social）的實時資料介面（API）。 據新華社此前報道，TMTG在7月時就曾洽談向華爾街金融機構出售該資料介面，計劃收取每月10萬美元的費用，使這些機構比公眾提前數毫秒獲知美國總統特朗普等人在“真實社交”平臺所發帖文。而最新公佈的細節顯示，除了每月高達10萬美元的標準訂閱費（三年長約優惠價為每月6萬美元）外，根據TMTG提交給美國證券交易委員會（SEC）的檔案，該API還附帶了追溯至2022年的歷史帖文庫，並支援全天候執行。 麥格恩稱：“市場本就會因‘真實社交’上的帖文產生波動。Truth API能夠提供該平臺上最能撼動市場的‘真實帖文’（Truths）的直接、授權、實時訂閱源，同時推動我們透過高毛利、可持續的收入流實現自研資產變現的戰略。” 目前，美國政府監察機構和國會民主黨人對這項服務提出了批評，並要求SEC介入調查。（第一財經）

【美國銀行宣佈未來一年向美國關鍵基礎設施投資2500億美元】美國銀行公佈一項 2500 億美元投資計劃，將在未來一年投向全美關鍵基礎設施領域，跟隨同業機構推動國內創新發展。該行週三釋出宣告表示，這筆資金將投向資料中心與算力、可再生能源發電、儲能、天然氣、電力傳輸、關鍵礦產及採礦等專案，旨在保障能源安全、帶動就業增長、提升經濟競爭力。該行全球基礎設施與可持續金融業務負責人、全球資本解決方案聯合負責人 Karen Fang 在採訪中稱：“缺少實體基礎設施，就很難為下一代維持我們的競爭力與領先地位。老舊基礎設施必須完成現代化改造。”這是美國各大銀行接連推出基礎設施投資計劃的最新舉措。摩根士丹利本週公佈規模 1.5 萬億美元的承諾，計劃未來十年在融資、籌資及顧問服務方面提供支援。

【大摩承認“站錯隊”、火速上調美股IT硬體評級】摩根士丹利一改此前謹慎立場，上調美國IT硬體行業評級，理由是記憶體晶片價格持續飆升的“晶片通脹”(chipflation)非但沒有扼殺需求，反而倒逼企業加速採購，形成一波“錯失採購恐懼症”的囤貨潮。不過，該行同時警示，當前行情主要為週期性驅動，一旦盈利上調週期見頂，將再度轉趨謹慎。摩根士丹利分析師埃裡克·伍德林(Erik Woodring)在最新報告中，將美國IT硬體行業展望從“謹慎”上調至“與大盤持平”，並直言不諱地承認其此前“在企業硬體交易上站錯了隊”。該行曾一度認為，創紀錄的元件價格通脹將迅速扼殺硬體支出的復甦，但現實恰恰相反。

### 2026-08-12 16:51:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bulgaria #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Bulgaria Trade Deficit Widens in June — Bulgaria’s trade deficit widened to EUR 1,010.7 million in June 2026 from EUR 575.9 million in the corresponding month a year earlier. Imports rose 22.1% year-on-year to EUR 5,175.5 million, driven by higher purchases from both the EU (9.6%) and non-EU countries (40.4%). Meanwhile, exports increased 13.7% to EUR 4,164.8 million, supported by sales to the EU (15.8%) and non-EU markets (10.1%). Over the first six months of the year, imports climbed 14.4% to EUR 29,381.2 million, with purchases rising from Kazakhstan (38.9%), Turkey (36.9%), and China (64.7%). Imports increased across raw materials excluding fuels (79.9%), mineral fuels, oils, and similar products (44.7%), and machinery, equipment, and vehicles (26.9%). Meanwhile, exports rose 8.5% to EUR 22,968.4 million, with shipments growing to China (55%), Serbia (14.1%), and the United Kingdom (35%). Sales increased for chemical substances and products (17.1%), and miscellaneous finished products (10%).

Lumber Hovers Near 3-Month Low — Lumber prices hovered around $580 per thousand board feet, remaining near a three-month low as reports of an interim trade deal between Canada and the US eased concerns over reduced supply. Canada’s minister responsible for US trade relations said the country remains committed to negotiations. Earlier, the US imposed tariffs on several Canadian lumber-related goods, although Canadian softwood lumber was excluded. Still, US demand for the Canadian lumber declined 1.7% in the first half of the year, with the American share of trade slipping slightly. Shipments to markets outside the US fell 3%. Meanwhile, weakened homebuying activity continued to pressure demand. Mortgage rates rose to their highest level in a year, according to Freddie Mac, while mortgage applications declined in the final weeks of July. Construction spending on lumber-intensive single-family housing also fell 3.3% year-on-year in June.

### 2026-08-12 16:46:31  #公司 #國際

【韓最高法院終審判決日企賠償二戰遭強徵勞工受害者】據韓聯社12日報道，韓國大法院（最高法院）當天就二戰中遭日本強徵勞工受害者遺屬對一家日本企業提起的索賠訴訟案作出終審判決，維持二審判決結果，要求被告賠償8000萬韓元（約合38萬元人民幣）。 韓國籍勞工受害者閔某的遺屬2019年4月對日本製鐵公司提起索賠訴訟。2022年2月，一審法院以遺屬行使損害賠償請求的權利已超過訴訟時效為由，判處原告敗訴。但二審法院於2024年9月推翻一審判決，要求被告向原告支付8000萬韓元賠償金。 日本1910年至1945年殖民統治朝鮮半島期間強徵大批勞工到日本做苦力。長期以來，韓國受害勞工及遺屬多次提起訴訟向日方索賠，但日本政府和相關企業一直以《韓日請求權協定》“已解決”索賠問題為由拒絕賠償。 2018年10月，韓國大法院在判處日本新日鐵住金公司（現為日本製鐵公司）賠償4名韓國籍二戰遭強徵勞工受害者每人1億韓元的判決中，明確認定，韓日1965年恢復邦交正常化時簽署的《韓日請求權協定》並不妨礙二戰時遭強徵的韓國籍勞工行使個人索賠請求權。(新華社)

【水利部持續滾動部署颱風“白海豚” 暴雨洪水防禦工作】8月12日上午，國家防總副總指揮、水利部部長李國英主持專題會商，滾動分析今年第13號颱風“白海豚”殘餘環流影響態勢，深入研判突出風險，進一步安排部署颱風暴雨洪水防禦工作。李國英指出，颱風“白海豚”雖已停止編號，但其殘餘環流與冷空氣結合，仍將造成大範圍持續性強降雨，持續影響太湖流域、長江中下游、淮河流域西部南部、黃河中游小花區間、海河流域南部、遼河流域東北部。預報8月12日至16日，太湖及周邊河網地區河流將持續維持超警；長江下游支流滁河、巢湖，漢江中下游幹流，淮河上中游幹流及支流淠河、洪汝河、沙潁河、渦河，海河流域衛河等主要河湖將出現明顯漲水過程，其中，沙潁河、洪汝河、滁河將發生編號洪水，淮河中游幹流、漢江中下游幹流接近警戒水位；暴雨區內部分中小河流可能發生超警以上洪水，山洪災害風險高。

【South Korea to require simulated trading for new single-stock leveraged ETF investors from Aug 19】South Korea will require new investors in single-stock leveraged exchange-traded funds to complete a simulated trading exercise, the Financial Services Commission said. New investors must complete at least five days of simulated trading totaling no less than five hours; the rule applies to domestic and overseas investors and takes effect August 19. The step is the latest tightening after a market crash that caused investor losses of tens of billions of dollars and follows criticism that leveraged products linked to Samsung Electronics and SK Hynix amplified KOSPI volatility. Regulators had earlier raised minimum cash margin for such trades to KRW30m (about $21k) and extended mandatory online training for new investors to three hours.

### 2026-08-12 16:38:38  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 2

Wheat Jumps as Black Sea Supply Risks Mount — Wheat prices surged more than 3% to above $6.50 per bushel on Wednesday, moving closer to the two-year high of $7.08 reached on July 22, as concerns over Black Sea supply disruptions intensified. Prices jumped after reports that major Ukrainian drone attacks had halted operations at a grain terminal in Novorossiysk, Russia’s leading Black Sea wheat export port. The attacks have raised fresh concerns over shipments during the peak export season for both Russia and Ukraine, with the two countries increasingly targeting each other’s vessels and logistics infrastructure. Russia’s wheat exports are expected to fall to their lowest level in nearly a decade in August, while consultancy IKAR has cut its 2026/27 export forecast by 500,000 tonnes to 44.5 million tonnes. Ukraine has also lowered its grain export outlook by up to 12%. However, weak international demand, lower Russian prices and alternative export routes could limit the impact on global supplies.

【華爾街對美聯儲9月是否加息爭執不下 通脹報告料定乾坤】華爾街對於美聯儲下月是否會加息分歧嚴重。不過有一點沒有爭議：週三公佈的通脹報告將在很大程度上影響美聯儲下一步行動。根據掉期市場交易情況，交易員目前預計美聯儲加息25個基點的機率約為50%。道明證券美國利率策略師Molly Brooks表示，如果通脹資料高於預期，加息機率可能大幅上升；如果資料再次偏弱，則會給決策者繼續觀望的空間。Brooks表示：“我們認為這份資料對9月決策至關重要。”她補充稱，市場反應可能並不對稱，通脹高於預期對加息機率的推動作用，可能遠大於通脹低於預期帶來的影響。市場等待通脹報告之際，美國國債週三變動不大。基準10年期美債收益率下跌1個基點至4.68%，30年期收益率降至5.23%。

【國際能源署：霍爾木茲海峽持續關閉衝擊全球石油市場】當地時間8月12日，總部位於巴黎的國際能源署釋出最新月度石油市場報告稱，受霍爾木茲海峽持續關閉、燃料價格高企以及中東局勢影響，全球石油供需均受到明顯衝擊。國際能源署預計，2026年全球石油需求將下降160萬桶/日，降幅較上月預測擴大51萬桶/日；全球石油供應預計下降430萬桶/日。報告顯示，7月全球石油供應增至1.015億桶/日，但仍較去年同期減少630萬桶/日，其中海灣地區仍有830萬桶/日產量處於中斷狀態。7月全球可觀測石油庫存驟降6900萬桶，自戰爭開始以來累計下降4.1億桶。國際能源署預計，今年第三季度全球石油市場供應缺口將達到180萬桶/日，是上月預測的兩倍以上。報告指出，隨著可用庫存緩衝迅速耗盡，重新開放霍爾木茲海峽的緊迫性進一步上升。7月國際油價劇烈波動，一度升至每桶105美元，目前北海即期原油價格約為每桶92美元。（央視新聞）

### 2026-08-12 16:27:21  #公司

【受颱風影響部分地區快遞運輸暫停，順豐、申通、韻達等集體回應】受颱風“白海豚”殘餘環流影響，多地迎來大範圍強降雨。截至8月12日9時，北京、河北、河南等五省市共有190處國省幹線臨時封閉。受此影響，部分地區快遞運輸暫停，派送時效延後。

中新經緯12日致電申通快遞客服，客服表示，暴雨確實影響了寄遞時效，各地寄遞情況根據當地雨情大小會有區別。

順豐速運客服則表示，江蘇部分城市和浙江舟山寄遞目前仍受到影響，浙江舟山暫停收寄，其他地區則是運輸會增加時間。同時該客服表示，目前順豐是“全產品加時0.5天”。

韻達快遞表示，總部第一時間啟動極端天氣防汛應急機制，成立跨部門應急工作小組，實時監測氣象預警、道路通行及全網網點運營狀態。同時，動態調整運輸路由，對受阻線路及時規劃繞行方案，靈活調配全網運力資源；對末端網點因汛情對派件產生較大影響的，省區結合實際情況最佳化相關運營管理安排。

圓通速遞表示，已第一時間啟動防颱防汛應急響應機制，滾動跟蹤颱風路徑、氣象預警和路網變化，統籌相關省區做好人員避險、場地排查、快件防護和運輸銜接等工作，並將各項應急措施逐級落實到集運中心和網點，全力守護人員安全、快件安全。

此外，中通快遞12日釋出異常天氣影響快件時效提醒稱，受暴雨影響，存在部分高速封閉、機場通行能力下降情況，相關區域進出快件時效較平日將有所延長，公司會密切關注天氣變化與交通狀況，合理調配資源，全力保障快件安全送達。

### 2026-08-12 16:22:41  #Trading Economics #Economy #Italy #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Italy July Inflation Revised Up to 2.9% — The annual inflation rate in Italy came at 2.9% in July 2026, revised up from a preliminary estimate of 2.8% but slightly down from 3.0% in June. It marked the second moderation since the rate climbed to a more than two-year high of 3.2% in May, as the temporary resumption of energy exports from the Middle East helped ease price pressures during the period. Non-regulated energy inflation, which is more responsive to movements in global benchmarks, slowed to 11.4% from 13.3% in June. Price growth also eased for unprocessed food products (3.6% vs 4.4%) and miscellaneous services (1.8% vs 2.5%). Conversely, inflation picked up for regulated energy (14.8% vs 9.2%), transport-related services (1.6% vs 1.1%), and recreational, cultural and personal care services (3.0% vs 2.7%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.3%, following a flat reading in June. Core inflation, which excludes energy eased to 1.8% from 1.9%.

### 2026-08-12 16:20:03  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #Brent #Impact bullish #WTI

【IEA: Despite demand drop, global oil supply shortfall to widen】The IEA said renewed escalation of Iran-related hostilities and maritime disruptions will hinder output recovery, leaving the global oil market with an estimated 1.8 mln bpd supply shortfall. It expects inventory draws this quarter to be more than double prior estimates and says global stocks have tightened again. The agency raised its forecast of this year’s global oil demand decline by nearly 50% to 1.6 mln bpd, the largest annual drop since 2020. The IEA warned the 2026 supply gap could be the biggest in five years but expects inventories to be rebuilt next year as the market swings back to surplus. It added that the US, Japan and Germany, after record strategic reserve releases in March, will need to replenish emergency stocks.

### 2026-08-12 16:11:48  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact mixed #Brent

【Strait of Hormuz exports remain disrupted; IEA sharply cuts 2026 global oil demand forecast】The IEA cut its global oil demand outlook, citing continued Strait of Hormuz export disruptions and sustained high fuel prices. It now expects 2026 demand to fall 1.56 mln b/d YoY — about 0.51 mln b/d lower than its prior forecast — to 103.29 mln b/d. The agency sees Q2 demand down 4.90 mln b/d YoY, with the decline narrowing to 2.80 mln b/d in the current quarter and reversing to growth of roughly 0.58 mln b/d in Q4. Assuming a de‑escalation between the US and Iran and gradual supply restoration, the IEA forecasts 2027 demand will rise 2.40 mln b/d to 105.7 mln b/d. It says restored flows through the Strait of Hormuz would convert an expected 2026 supply shortfall of about 4.30 mln b/d into a 2027 surplus of roughly 8.30 mln b/d, shifting the market from a 1.30 mln b/d deficit in 2026 to a 4.60 mln b/d surplus in 2027.

### 2026-08-12 16:08:13  #其他

【IEA月報：預計2026年全球石油供應平均下降430萬桶/日】8月12日，國際能源署（IEA）釋出石油市場報告。報告顯示，2026年全球石油需求預計將下降160萬桶/日，較上月報告中的預估增加51萬桶/日，原因是霍爾木茲海峽持續關閉以及燃料價格高企繼續拖累石油消費。2027年全球石油需求預計將擴大240萬桶/日。7月全球石油供應增加240萬桶/日，達到1.015億桶/日，但仍較去年同期水平低630萬桶/日，其中海灣地區仍有830萬桶/日的產量處於關停狀態。IEA表示，7月和8月初再次爆發的敵對行動和海上干擾破壞了恢復努力，使2026年第三季度的石油供應預測較上月報告下調170萬桶/日。目前預計2026年全球石油供應平均下降430萬桶/日，明年將反彈830萬桶/日，達到1.103億桶/日。

### 2026-08-12 16:05:51  #國際

【聯合國專家：歐洲持續極端高溫非厄爾尼諾所致】聯合國世界氣象組織11日指出，當前，全球變暖速度最快的歐洲部分地區正遭受今夏第五輪熱浪侵襲，經歷乾旱、野火與歷史性高溫。此種極端反常天氣由全球變暖、持續高壓系統等多重因素造成，並非由厄爾尼諾現象所致。世界氣象組織氣候資訊負責人約翰·肯尼迪當天在新聞釋出會上表示，歐洲當前的高溫模式反映了北半球持續高壓系統與氣候變暖之間的相互作用。高壓系統鎖定暖空氣，減少雲層覆蓋，阻止冷空氣進入，使熱量在多地積聚數天或數週。他特別指出，目前熱帶太平洋地區的氣候條件為“中性”，因此今年的歐洲熱浪並非由厄爾尼諾現象所致。（新華社 ）

FTSE MIB Trades Little Changed — The FTSE MIB traded flat to slightly higher on Wednesday, extending the subdued session from the previous day, as markets remained cautious over the prospects of a deal to reopen the Strait of Hormuz. Markets also awaited the US CPI report, which could offer further insight into inflation trends and influence expectations for the Fed’s upcoming policy decisions. Tech-related stocks led gains, with Prysmian advancing 2.6% amid a broader global tech rally, while STMicroelectronics gained 0.7%. Another notable performer was Unipol, which added 1.1%, while Eni rose 0.2% after agreeing to take a 40% stake in Uruguay’s Off-6 oil and gas exploration block, with drilling of the first well scheduled for 2027. On the downside, DiaSorin fell 3.7%, while luxury names such as Moncler and Brunello Cucinelli lost over 1% each. On the data front, Italy’s annual inflation rate was revised up to 2.9% in July from a preliminary 2.8%, while monthly inflation was revised up to 0.3% from 0.2%.

India 10Y Yield Steady on Oil, CPI — The yield on India’s 10-year G-Sec hovered around 6.78%, trading in a tight range as elevated oil prices restrained demand for government bonds ahead of key India and US inflation data. Brent crude rose 0.8% to $89.6 per barrel, as uncertainty over the Middle East conflict kept oil prices elevated and raised inflation concerns. Meanwhile, India’s July inflation data, due later Wednesday, is expected to show inflation rising to 4.50% from 4.38% in June, while a hotter-than-expected US reading could revive Fed rate-hike bets and put upward pressure on Indian yields by narrowing the yield premium over US bonds. However, expectations for further RBI rate hikes have eased since the central bank kept rates unchanged last week and lowered its inflation forecasts. Strong foreign inflows and ample liquidity have also supported bonds, with the RBI’s diaspora deposit scheme attracting over $36.7 billion as of July 17, while the daily average cash surplus exceeded INR 3 trillion in August.

### 2026-08-12 15:46:16  #Trading Economics #Markets #Euro Area #Stock Market #Importance 2

European Stocks Lack Direction — Both the STOXX 50 and STOXX 600 hovered around the flatline near record levels on Wednesday, as traders refrained from making major bets ahead of the US CPI report, which is expected to provide fresh clues on price pressures and help shape expectations for the Fed's next steps. Rising oil prices also kept sentiment cautious. Meanwhile, the earnings season is drawing to a close, although several companies continued to report results. ABN AMRO surged more than 5% after beating market expectations across the board, while Vestas jumped more than 18% after reporting higher revenue. The tech sector was among the top performers, benefiting from a broader global tech rally after CoreWeave reported better-than-expected sales growth. ASML Holding rose 2%, Infineon Technologies gained 1.6%, Siemens Energy advanced 3.5%, and Rheinmetall climbed 1.3%. Luxury stocks, meanwhile, came under pressure, with LVMH falling 1.8%, Hermes declining 1.5%, and Kering dropping 2.9%.

【百誠醫藥旗下百倫檢測中標昆藥集團採購專案】據百誠醫藥8月12日訊息，近日，百誠醫藥旗下全資子公司杭州百倫檢測技術有限公司（簡稱“百倫檢測”）中標華潤醫藥集團有限公司旗下昆藥集團股份有限公司（簡稱“昆藥集團”）的“基因毒性雜質評估單價框架採購專案”。本次合作聚焦於藥品基因毒性雜質的評估服務。定量結構—活性關係作為國際通用的計算毒理學技術，能夠依託化合物分子結構快速預判其潛在致突變性與毒性風險。在此次合作中，百倫檢測將嚴格對標ICHM7、NMPA、FDA及EMA等國內外主流藥監指導原則，為昆藥集團提供涵蓋工藝雜質、降解雜質與工藝殘留的前置化致突變風險智慧預測與分級判定，協助企業科學制定雜質控制限度。

### 2026-08-12 15:10:30  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

UK Natural Gas Prices Rebound — UK natural gas prices rose above 148 pence a therm on Wednesday, rebounding from the previous session’s losses, as doubts grew over the likelihood of an agreement to resume energy flows through the Strait of Hormuz. Supply concerns persisted despite Pakistan’s defense minister saying that the US and Iran were “close to some sort of arrangement” over the waterway, while negotiations between Tehran and Oman were also reportedly making significant progress. Both Washington and Tehran had taken a firmer stance, clouding the outlook for a swift reopening of the strait. The ongoing disruption to shipping has severely constrained LNG shipments, with cargoes from major exporter Qatar significantly delayed. Analysts expect European gas prices to maintain a firm floor until storage levels show clearer signs of building ahead of the heating season. Hot weather across Europe is also boosting electricity demand for cooling, adding further pressure to the market.

【平安證券：維持甘李藥業“推薦”評級，創新管線穩步推進】平安證券研報指出，甘李藥業加速海外戰略佈局，創新管線穩步推進。公司日前已與跨國生物製藥公司美納里尼集團（TheMenariniGroup，以下簡稱“Menarini”）簽署獨家許可協議。博凡格魯肽是甘李藥業自主研發的1類創新藥，該藥的核心競爭力在於其每兩週一次給藥的機制，相比傳統GLP-1RA周製劑注射頻次減少約50%，有望提升患者用藥依從性，助力患者長期體重管理。考慮到公司自2026年1月起增值稅由簡易徵收（3%）變更為一般計稅方法（13%），稅制口徑變化影響收入確認，但是公司加速海外佈局，創新管線陸續進入收穫期，調整2026-2028年盈利預測為歸母淨利潤14.53、16.89、24.54億元（原26、27年預測為15.30、18.34億元）。考慮到公司藉助胰島素續約加速國產替代步伐，海外佈局持續加速，維持“推薦”評級。

### 2026-08-12 14:59:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Inflation Rate #Importance 3

US Inflation Rate Expected to Slow for 2nd Month — The annual inflation rate in the US is expected to slow for a second consecutive month to 3.4% in July 2026, from 3.5% in June, easing further from the 2023 high of 4.2% reached in May. On a monthly basis, the CPI is forecast to rise 0.1%, rebounding from a 0.4% decline in June, which marked the first monthly drop since May 2020. Gasoline prices are expected to have fallen nearly 3%, while airfares and jet fuel prices are also likely to have declined. New and used car prices, meanwhile, could see a slight uptick. Core consumer prices are expected to rise 0.2%, following a flat reading in June, while the annual core inflation rate is seen easing to 2.5% from 2.6% in the previous month. That would mark the smallest annual increase since February. Overall, the CPI report is likely to point to a further cooling in energy-related price pressures that intensified in the months immediately following the start of the US war with Iran.

TAIEX Hits Near 4-Week High, Ends 0.9% Higher — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, advanced 397 points, or 0.9%, to close at 45,518 on Wednesday, extending gains for the third consecutive session, mainly buoyed by electronic technology, technology services, and producer manufacturing. The broader index lingered at its highest level since July 16, as traders capitalized on the upward momentum in the tech sector. Electronic technology stocks gained 1.1%, with TSMC, the world's largest contract chipmaker and a company that accounts for more than 40% of the market's total value, climbing 0.8% after a 0.6% gain a day earlier. Hon Hai Precision Industry surged 2.5%, while Unimicron rose 0.8%. Meanwhile, Accton Technology Corp. and Quanta Computer jumped 3.8% and 3.2%, respectively. However, a decline on Wall Street overnight limited gains, as rising oil prices raised questions about the Fed's monetary policy at its upcoming meeting, while traders awaited the release of US inflation data later today.

【美銀：近年美股財富效應顯現，更多55+人群選擇“解甲歸田”】美銀近日的一份研究備忘錄援引美國勞工統計局統計資料指出，2020年2月，美國55歲及以上人群勞動力參與率為40.3%；到2026年7月，該數值已經降至36.9%。美銀全球研究部董事總經理、美國經濟學家Aditya Bhave表示：“我們認為這和標普500指數過去兩年累計超35%的漲幅有關。”在他看來，股市大幅上漲，讓不少還在糾結何時退場的人，更容易做出退休的選擇。Bhave並未將該現象全部歸因於股市，他表示：“沒有哪一個單一因果因素可以完整解釋該現象。但很明顯，過去兩年股市表現亮眼，如果算上2020年以來的行情走勢，標普500指數已經實現累計翻倍以上漲幅，這種財富增長會促使一部分人選擇退休，他們會覺得‘我不必再繼續工作了’。”

### 2026-08-12 14:45:19  #MKT News #Star Stocks #Palantir #Market Themes #AI Revolution #NET #Impact bullish #PLTR #DDOG

【Goldman Sachs: software stocks begin to realize AI gains】Goldman Sachs TMT trader Peter Callahan says the post-earnings AI narrative within software is shifting: fears that generative AI would erode traditional software moats are yielding to a view that data infrastructure and developer-tools vendors are moving from an AI headwind to a tailwind. Cloudflare, Palantir, Datadog, Twilio and Atlassian have drawn greater investor attention; traditional SaaS application vendors still need to prove clear AI benefit. Callahan notes AI commercialisation is extending from model training into inference, agents and automation. Cloudflare disclosed non‑human traffic now exceeds human traffic and said machine‑generated requests should grow rapidly if current trends persist. That suggests AI can be demand‑accretive for platforms that carry data, APIs, network traffic, security and developer tools, since more agents and higher call frequency can create direct revenue opportunities. Software is therefore bifurcating: AI upside increasingly depends on whether a company sits at the application layer or the infrastructure layer and on its ability to monetise AI traffic.

ASX 200 Finishes in the Red — The ASX 200 fell 41 points, or 0.45%, to close at 9,209 on Wednesday, erasing the prior session’s modest gains as sharp losses in manufacturing, consumer non-durables, healthcare, and retail trade dragged the index to its weakest level in a week. Sentiment soured after the Reserve Bank of Australia held the cash rate steady at 4.35% on Tuesday but warned further tightening could follow if inflation lingers. Wall Street’s overnight slump added pressure, with escalating Middle East tensions and renewed attacks near key shipping routes stoking energy supply fears. Commonwealth Bank slipped 0.7% after reporting record annual cash earnings but flagged a sharp slowdown in mortgage demand post-tax changes. Rio Tinto eased 0.2% even as it secured government support to offset soaring energy costs. ASX tumbled 2.8% after a shareholder moved to file a Federal Court application against the bourse operator. Bucking the trend, AGL Energy surged 6.0% on stronger-than-expected 2027 earnings guidance.

### 2026-08-12 14:40:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Norway #Consumer Confidence #Importance 2

Norway Consumer Pessimism Eases in Q3 — Norway’s adjusted consumer confidence indicator improved to -16.6 in Q3 2026, up from a downwardly revised -18.1 in Q2. The improvement came alongside less pessimistic views of the country’s economic outlook, with expectations for the national economy over the next year improving to -24.6 from -34.9 in Q2 and assessments of its performance over the past year rising to -37.1 from -47.1. Households were also more optimistic about their own finances, with expectations for personal finances over the next year turning slightly positive at 0.8 from -3.5, while assessments of their financial situation over the past year improved to -2.6 from -9.4. Sentiment toward major purchases also strengthened to -8.7 from -15.8, while households said they would prioritize repaying loans or saving and investing if their financial situation improved, with both responses reaching record highs since the survey began in 1992. Meanwhile, the unadjusted indicator rose to -14.4 from -22.1.

China Car Sales Drop 0.3% in July — China's total vehicle sales fell 0.3% from a year earlier in July 2026, following a 3.2% decline in the previous month, according to data from the China Association of Automobile Manufacturers (CAAM). New energy vehicle (NEV) sales soared 23.7% year-on-year. For the first seven months of the year, vehicle sales dropped 3.7% year-on-year, compared with a 12.0% increase in the same period last year, while NEV sales climbed 9.6%. Last year, China's vehicle sales grew 9.4% year-on-year to a record high of 34.4 million units, accelerating from a 4.5% rise in 2024, while NEV sales surged 28.2%.

### 2026-08-12 14:28:13  #Trading Economics #Economy #Romania #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Romania Inflation Falls to One-Year Low — The annual inflation rate in Romania eased to 8.2% in July 2026 from 10.4% in June, remaining above market expectations of 7.9% but marking the lowest reading since July 2025. Inflation for non-food goods posted the sharpest slowdown, easing to 7.9% from 12.3% in June, largely due to a steep moderation in electricity price growth (2.9% vs 35.2%). Food inflation also slowed (5.0% vs 5.8%), reflecting softer price growth for vegetables, fruits, meat and meat products, milk and dairy products, and sugar. Meanwhile, service inflation was unchanged at 13.7%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.58% in July, accelerating from 0.06% in June. Meanwhile, the HICP-based annual inflation rate eased to 8.2% from 9.2% in the previous month.

【MS downgrades Tencent Music US PT to $10.1; competitive outlook needs more clarity】Morgan Stanley cut its price target on Tencent Music (TME.N) to $10.1 from $10.5 and maintained a market-weight rating. The bank said synergies from the Himalaya acquisition are uncertain and could be a swing factor amid a prolonged competitive and earnings downgrade cycle. Accelerated share buybacks offer partial downside support but clearer visibility on competition is required. Morgan Stanley trimmed Tencent Music 2026–2028 EPS forecasts by 3–7% to reflect weaker subscription revenue. At current levels the stock trades at roughly 10x 2027E P/E and 6.6x ex-cash P/E; any valuation rerating depends on an improvement in the competitive landscape.

### 2026-08-12 14:15:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sweden #Household Spending MoM #Importance 1

Sweden Household Spending Rebounds in June — Household spending in Sweden jumped 1.6% month-on-month in June 2026, rebounding from a revised 0.3% decline in the previous month. It marked the biggest increase since January, driven by a rebound in spending on personal care, social protection, and miscellaneous goods and services (5.9% vs -3.9% in May), transportation (5.4% vs -4.1%), recreation, sports, and culture (4.9% vs -1.5%), healthcare (3.9% vs -4.4%), furniture, household equipment, and routine home maintenance (3.8% vs -2.3%), and alcoholic beverages, tobacco, and drugs (1.6% vs -2.1%). Consumers also spent more on information and communication (5.3% vs 1.3%), as well as clothing and footwear (3.5% vs 0.4%). Meanwhile, expenditure on food and non-alcoholic beverages (0.5% vs 0.7%) and housing and utilities (0.4% vs 1.6%) slowed. On a yearly basis, household consumption rose 4.3%, following a revised 2.0% increase in May.

### 2026-08-12 14:14:33  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Palladium Holds Near 2-Month High — Palladium futures climbed toward $1,370 per ounce, remaining near more than two-month highs as reduced expectations for a Federal Reserve rate hike and broader strength across precious metals supported demand. Markets price in about a 50% chance of a September Fed hike, down from 60% before last week’s weaker-than-expected US jobs report, with lower rates generally supporting non-yielding precious metals. Meanwhile, heightened geopolitical tensions supported precious metals, as attacks on shipping by the US and Yemen’s Iran-aligned Houthis and uncertainty over the Iran conflict kept safe-haven demand elevated. On the supply front, concerns over disruptions in South Africa and uncertainty over Russian exports continue to support prices, with extended maintenance at South African processing facilities reducing refined PGM output amid tight inventories. Over the past month, palladium has risen 9.58% and is up 21.67% year-on-year.

### 2026-08-12 14:10:31  #公司 #市場 #其他 #A股

【宇樹打新不只看運氣 黃牛收購價漲到410元/股】8月11日下午，宇樹科技打新結果陸續出爐，網上發行中籤率為0.0181%。這意味著，大約每5500個申購號碼裡，只有1個能中籤。這個機率，是長鑫科技中籤率的1/26。滬深打新規則下，股民幾乎只能拼運氣，但在數載難逢的“大肉籤”面前，部分打新者依然有提高中籤率的招數。例如，頂格申購，用滿12個配號；提早收集符合科創板資質、有滬市交易記錄的股票賬號，以量取勝。一位股民則加入了一個“共享打新群”：群主組織數百人一起申購，中籤後，群主按每次收益的20%—30%抽成，中籤者可以額外拿總收益的10%，剩下的則給每個參與申購的人按貢獻度平分，例如貢獻配號數量。“這樣就把小機率賺大錢、拼運氣的事，變成了更多人大機率賺小錢的生意。”部分網民在社交媒體上討論，宇樹科技上市後是否會破發。該股民透露，上市前，市場上已有黃牛在高價收宇樹科技的股票，8月10日報出的收購價已經漲到410元/股，比發行價150.80元/股高出170%。（經濟觀察報）

### 2026-08-12 14:09:46  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

German July Inflation Confirmed at 3-Month High of 2.8% — Germany's annual inflation rate accelerated to 2.8% in July 2026, confirming preliminary estimates, up from 2.3% in June. It was the highest reading since April, driven by a sharp rise in energy inflation to 8.3% from 3.4%, as renewed Middle East tensions pushed oil prices higher. Meanwhile, services inflation eased to 2.9% from 3.1%, while food inflation remained unchanged at 0.4%. Core inflation, which excludes energy and unprocessed food, inched down to 2.4% from 2.5%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.8% in July, rebounding from a 0.3% fall in June. The EU-harmonized inflation rate also accelerated to 2.8%, its highest level in three months and well above the European Central Bank's 2.0%

### 2026-08-12 14:09:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Machine Tool Orders #Importance 1

Japan Machine Tool Orders Surge 50.4% in July — Japan’s machine tool orders climbed by 50.4% year-on-year to JPY 193,102 million in July 2026, following a 52.8% surge in the previous month. The increase reflected robust demand across both domestic and overseas markets. Foreign orders rose 50.5% year-on-year to JPY 139,864 million, following a 56.0% increase in June, while domestic orders advanced 50.2% to JPY 53,238 million, after rising 45.5% in the previous month. On a monthly basis, total machine tool orders declined 5.1% in July, reversing sharply from a 15% increase in June. For the first seven months of the year, machine tool orders totaled JPY 1,248,243 million, up 37.8% from the corresponding period a year earlier, with foreign and domestic orders increasing 42.3% and 26.5%, respectively.

【上半年中國人形機器人出貨量全球佔比超97%】最新行業研究資料顯示，今年上半年全球人形機器人出貨量總計約1.91萬臺，是去年同期5100臺的三倍多，中國人形機器人出貨量全球佔比達97%以上。在市場份額方面，智元機器人出貨量為8400臺，全球佔比44%，位列第一；宇樹科技的出貨量為5900臺，全球佔比31%，這兩家公司的出貨量遠超美國人形機器人頭部企業。另外三家中國企業銀河通用、優必選和樂聚出貨量全球佔比分別為5%、4%和3%。在用途方面，被用於工業和商業的人形機器人的數量超全球總出貨量七成，去年同期這一比例約為50%。預計今年全年全球人形機器人的出貨總量將增長至約6萬臺，到2030年將達50萬臺。 (央視財經)

### 2026-08-12 14:00:39  #行業 #其他 #焦點

【連續兩個月突破100萬輛 我國汽車出口延續強勁增長態勢】記者今天（12日）從中國汽車工業協會獲悉，7月份我國汽車出口表現亮眼，延續強勁增長態勢。 最新發布的資料顯示，今年7月份，汽車出口104.3萬輛，環比增長0.6%，同比增長81.3%。其中，新能源汽車出口55.3萬輛，環比增長5.7%，同比增長145.5%。記者注意到，自今年4月、5月連續兩個月汽車出口超過90萬輛後，6月、7月出口邁上新臺階，出口連續兩個月突破100萬輛。不僅如此，新能源汽車出口佔比已連續兩個月突破百分之五十。 中國汽車工業協會相關負責人表示，中國汽車出口之所以能夠保持高速增長，主要是中國擁有全球最完整的汽車產業鏈。從鋰礦加工、電池製造、電機電控到整車組裝，不僅產能充足、成本可控，而且供應鏈抗風險能力強。此外，我國汽車產業的智慧化形成了核心競爭力，智慧駕駛、座艙互動等功能能夠快速迭代，精準適配海外使用者的需求。 中國汽車工業協會副秘書長陳士華表示，綜合來看，當前我國汽車市場呈現出“內需承壓、外貿強勁”的特徵，出口正在成為穩住行業大盤的關鍵力量和核心增長引擎。

### 2026-08-12 13:40:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Finland #Current Account #Importance 1

Finland Current Account Surplus Largest on Record — Finland's current account surplus widened to EUR 2,830 million in June 2026 from EUR 1,282 million in the corresponding month of the previous year. This marked the largest current account surplus on record, largely as the goods account surplus climbed sharply to EUR 2,797 million in June from EUR 1,063 million a year earlier. At the same time, the primary income surplus increased to EUR 903 million from EUR 744 million in June 2025. Meanwhile, a wider deficit was recorded in the services account, which expanded to EUR 693 million from EUR 369 million, while the secondary income shortfall also rose to EUR 178 million from EUR 155 million.

【美國出售與委內瑞拉有關的油輪 此前該油輪在公海被扣押】據買家稱，美國政府已出售一艘與委內瑞拉有關的油輪。該油輪於今年1月被美國海岸警衛隊扣押，此前該油輪在大西洋上經歷了一場驚心動魄的追逐。這艘影子船隊船隻由此獲准拆解報廢。這艘名為“貝拉1號”（Bella 1）的油輪曾於去年年底逃離美國在加勒比海的封鎖，並因頻繁更改船名、在船體上噴塗俄羅斯國旗以尋求庇護而遭到當局數週的追捕。最終，該船在冰島附近被扣押。買家環球營銷系統公司（Global Marketing Systems，簡稱GMS）的運營總監約格什·雷哈尼（Yogesh Rehani）表示，這艘油輪——現名為“時代號”（Era）——已於上週晚些時候獲准拆解，同時獲准拆解的還有另一艘被列入黑名單並被扣押的阿芙拉型油輪“利萊奧號”（Lileo）。此前，美國財政部已為這兩艘油輪頒發了拆解許可證。海事資料庫Equasis顯示，GMS位於美國的子公司目前是這兩艘油輪的所有者。

Malaysia Retail Sales Growth Slows in June — Retail sales in Malaysia rose 6.6% year-on-year in June 2026, slowing from a 7.2% increase in the previous month. Growth moderated in retail sales in non-specialised stores (7.0% vs 8.2% in May), as well as sales of other household equipment in specialised stores (3.2% vs 3.4%), automotive fuel in specialised stores (10.8% vs 12.8%), and food, beverages and tobacco in specialised stores (6.6% vs 7.2%). On the other hand, sales grew at a faster pace for other goods in specialised stores (7.0% vs 6.7%) and information and communication equipment in specialised stores (4.7% vs 3.5%). On a monthly basis, retail sales edged up 0.2%, easing from a 1.3% increase in the previous month.

### 2026-08-12 12:29:00  #Trading Economics #Markets #Indonesia #Currency #Importance 1

Rupiah Extends Weakness Ahead of U.S. Inflation Data — The Indonesian rupiah extended its decline, hovering near IDR 17,870 per U.S. dollar on Wednesday, as the dollar index edged higher ahead of U.S. inflation data that could shape expectations for the Fed’s next policy move. Locally, weaker fundamentals weighed on sentiment, with retail sales and consumer mood staying subdued in July. Focus now turns to Bank Indonesia’s policy meeting next week, its first since Perry Warjiyo stepped down last month, with acting Governor Destry Damayanti nominated as sole candidate to lead the central bank. BI raised its policy rate by a total of 100bps in May and June to support the rupiah. Meanwhile, contained inflation should provide room for policy flexibility, with Indonesia's inflation rate hitting a three-month low of 2.88% in July, within the central bank's target band. The rupiah also found some support from hopes of policy continuity, as lawmakers are expected to approve Destry’s appointment smoothly, likely after Independence Day celebrations.

TAIEX Stays Near 4-Week High on Tech Sector Boost — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, climbed 343 points, or 0.8%, to 45,464 around noon on Wednesday, extending gains for the third consecutive session, mainly buoyed by electronic technology and producer manufacturing. Rupee Extends Losses Amid Oil Surge — The Indian rupee hovered around 95.4 per dollar, remaining under pressure as rising oil prices and weaker Asian currencies weighed on sentiment. The rupee has extended losses for another session since breaking past the 95 per dollar level last Wednesday, as uncertainty over the end of the US-Iran conflict has driven oil prices higher. Brent crude has risen 7% this week and was approaching $90 a barrel, prompting importers to step up hedging and increasing dollar demand, which has offset positive sentiment from RBI interventions. The central bank has been selling dollars through state-run banks in both trading sessions this week, with traders saying the rupee would likely have weakened further without the support. Meanwhile, US inflation data due later Wednesday could provide clues on whether the Federal Reserve will raise interest rates next month, with markets currently split on the prospect of a hike.

【我國主導的生命科學領域國際學術期刊《Vita》紙質刊首期釋出】今天，我國主導的生命科學領域《Vita》系列期刊紙質刊首期釋出。“Vita”一詞源自拉丁語，中文為“生命”之意。《Vita》系列期刊由主刊《Vita》和系列子刊組成，依託“生命科學開放聯盟”相關高校和機構共同建設。生命科學開放聯盟由內地與港澳15家高水平高校和科研機構共同發起，於2025年8月12日在香港正式成立，目前由39所內地與港澳高校和機構共同組成，聯盟將高水平科技期刊、國際化科研資料開放平臺等作為重要的建設內容。《Vita》系列期刊採用“主刊+子刊”的辦刊模式，學術水平上對標三大國際頂刊及其子刊，打造我國生命科學領域自主一流期刊系列。該期刊將創新建立“以文評刊”的科學評價體系，透過對各刊所載原創論文學術水平的鑑定來評估期刊學術水平，擺脫對SCI等引證指標的片面依賴，引導期刊迴歸學術本質，推動營造良好學術生態。（央視新聞）

【Ukraine strikes Russian refinery; Russia imports gasoline from India for first time】Russia has started importing gasoline from India after Ukrainian attacks on Russian refineries caused severe domestic fuel shortages, shipping data firm Kpler said. Kpler flagged this as Russia’s first import of automotive gasoline from a South Asian country: the initial India-origin cargo arrived on Aug. 5, and more shipments may follow. The fuel was carried on tankers linked to Russia and was transshipped near Egypt before moving to Russia. Kpler chief analyst Sumit Ritolia said Indian supplies, together with continued imports from Belarus and other nearby markets, underline the severity of Russia’s domestic gasoline supply-demand imbalance and reflect refinery run-rate cuts reshaping its refined-products trade flows.

### 2026-08-12 11:33:49  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bangladesh #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Bangladesh Inflation Rate at 8-Month Low — The annual inflation rate in Bangladesh rose to 8.32% in July 2026, easing from 9.16% in the previous month. This reading marked the lowest level since November 2025, as inflation slowed the most in food and non-alcoholic beverages (7.16% vs 8.60% in June), transport (9.34% vs 10.10%), housing and utilities (9.48% vs 9.63%), health (3.90% vs 4.28%), communication (6.90% vs 9.76%), recreation and culture (8.80% vs 9.80%), and education (8.73% vs 9.11%). Meanwhile, inflation remained elevated for restaurants and hotels (12.57% vs 11.70%), clothing and footwear (9.75% vs 9.64%), and furnishings, household equipment, and routine household maintenance (6.58% vs 6.43%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 1.44%, accelerating from 0.34% in June and marking the strongest monthly gain since October 2025. Bangladesh remains among the countries most vulnerable to disruptions related to the Middle East conflict due to its heavy reliance on imported fuel and limited reserve supplies.

### 2026-08-12 11:18:30  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #Market Regions #Japan & APAC #USDJPY #Impact bullish

【Bessent-Takaichi split over BOJ policy could undermine yen rebound】Analysts say a fundamental split between US Treasury Secretary Bessent and Japanese Prime Minister Takaichi on Bank of Japan policy could weaken recent US-Japan efforts to push the yen higher. Over the past year Bessent has signalled tighter monetary policy is crucial to addressing yen weakness; Takaichi has warned rapid, large rate increases could derail Japan's recovery. Since Takaichi took office in October the BOJ has hiked twice but the policy rate remains low at 1%. Last month the US carried out its first yen intervention since 1998 while the BOJ stood pat — a mixed policy signal that contrasts with historical experience that reshaping market expectations typically requires sustained, forceful action. Peter Vassallo, portfolio manager at BNP Paribas Asset Management US, called the episode a "golden opportunity" missed.

### 2026-08-12 11:14:41  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution #NVDA.O #Impact bullish #MSFT.O #AMZN.O

【Societe Generale: AI CapEx to accelerate in H2, peak could be H1 2027】Societe Generale strategist Manish Kabra’s team says recent extreme swings in semiconductors and momentum trades reflect concentrated de-leveraging and hedge fund unwind, not a synchronous weakening of AI fundamentals. They assess South Korea’s most acute de-leveraging phase is nearing an end and US momentum strategies have seen large-scale reductions. Hyperscaler cloud businesses remain in a strong upcycle in the latest results cycle; order backlogs are up about 15% and have pushed AI capital spending roughly 10% higher. The team expects AI CapEx to continue accelerating into H2 2026, with a potential peak in H1 2027. Societe Generale advises distinguishing trading-driven de-leveraging—which has cooled—from the still-expanding industrial CapEx cycle; until hyperscaler free cash flow materially improves, hardware, infrastructure and supply-chain suppliers that directly capture AI CapEx are likely more direct beneficiaries than high-spend customers.

Palm Oil Retreats on Ample Supplies, Profit-Taking — Malaysian palm oil futures eased, slipping below MYR 4,720 per tonne as profit-taking set in after a two-week high. Losses tracked declines in edible oils on the Dalian exchange and were compounded by signs of ample supply: July inventories rose 3.32% to 2.63 million tonnes, while output surged 9.41% to 1.79 million tonnes. Softer Chinese inflation data underscored weak demand in the world’s top edible oil importer, further weighing on sentiment. Still, downside was cushioned by a weaker ringgit and firmer soyoil prices on the Chicago exchange. In top buyer India, festive-season demand expectations lent support after July imports hit a ten-month peak. Export prospects brightened as cargo surveyors estimated shipments rose between 2.6% and 14.8% in the first ten days of August. Meanwhile, stronger crude oil prices added a tailwind, with Middle East supply concerns, heightened by attacks on two ships and uncertainty over a U.S.–Iran peace deal, bolstering the broader commodity complex.

STI Pulls Back from Record High on Profit-Taking — Singapore's stocks fell 36 points, or 0.6%, to 5,718 in Wednesday morning trade, erasing gains from the previous three sessions, as traders took profits after the index hit a record high a day earlier. The STI closed at 5,754 on Tuesday, buoyed by strong economic data following an upward revision to Q2 GDP growth to 5.9%. The broader index tracked a decline on Wall Street overnight, as rising oil prices raised concerns over inflation and expectations of an interest rate hike. Traders were cautious ahead of the release of US inflation data later today for clues about the Fed's monetary policy decisions at its upcoming meeting. However, fresh data capped the fall, as Singapore's current account surplus widened in Q2 from the same period in 2025. Communication, technology services, and finance mainly weighed on the index, with the biggest laggards being Jardine Matheson Holdings (-2.2%), Jardine Cycle & Carriage (-1.0%), OCBC (-0.9%), and Hongkong Land Holdings (-0.8%).

【中信建投：二季度煤價同比上漲 部分割槽域月度電價回升】中信建投釋出研報稱，火電方面，2026年第2季度，動力煤市場價同比上漲，廣東、江蘇等區域受電力供需偏緊、一次能源價格抬升等因素的影響，月度交易電價有所回升，部分對沖燃料成本的上升。同時，考慮到年度長協電量佔比較高，儘管月度電價回升，該行預期火電26Q2~Q3業績仍可能承壓。綠電方面，受新能源消納壓力較大疊加136號文推動新能源全面入市影響，2026上半年業績及估值均承壓。電力供需方面，受火電機組核准及投產節奏的影響，2027年全國火電新增裝機增速有望放緩，疊加一次能源價格的回升，27年長協電價有望企穩回升。綜合而言，電力板塊二季度業績壓力較大，江浙等用電增速較高區域的火電經營情況有望逐步改善。

The future industries category covers six areas: artificial and embodied intelligence, hydrogen energy, bio-manufacturing, quantum technologies, next-generation information networks and brain-like intelligence/brain–computer interfaces, and specifically highlights investment in humanoid robots, general-purpose large models, full-chain hydrogen technologies, bio-breeding, quantum computing and 6G mobile communications.

### 2026-08-12 10:39:31  #市場

【阿里雲：靈駿真武M890超節點例項上線，可承載十萬億引數模型 】阿里雲宣佈靈駿真武 M890超節點例項上線，首批已在烏蘭察布地域開售。企業級客戶無需自建機房，在雲上即可開通64卡高速互聯算力單元，最高可承載十萬億引數級MoE大模型推理。據悉，阿里雲靈駿真武M890超節點是國內首個成功執行超2萬億引數大模型的超節點形態算力，Kimi K3和Qwen3.8 Max均已透過該例項對外提供服務。超節點例項支援FP8/FP4低精度計算，透過 ICN Switch 1.0晶片Scale-up互聯規模由 16卡拉昇至 64卡，卡間互聯提升至 800GB/s。在智慧駕駛、具身智慧等訓練場景中，相比上一代真武810E，訓練效能提升三倍。支撐該超節點叢集的是阿里雲統一底座——靈駿智算平臺。依託HPN 8.0訓推一體網路，單叢集最高支援 13 萬卡異構算力混布，並可擴充套件至百萬卡級。（新浪科技）

US 10-Year Yield Edges Lower — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note eased to around 4.68% on Wednesday, extending its decline into a second session as investors positioned ahead of key inflation data that could provide fresh guidance on the Federal Reserve’s policy outlook. The consumer price index is due later today, with producer inflation figures scheduled for Thursday. Markets remain split over the prospect of a 25-basis-point Fed rate hike in September after the central bank left rates unchanged in July, while firmer oil prices continue to bolster expectations for a hawkish stance. On Tuesday, Chicago Fed President Austan Goolsbee said policymakers remain more focused on the risk of persistently elevated inflation than potential weakness in the labor market. Meanwhile, investors continued to monitor prospects for a US-Iran agreement to reopen the Strait of Hormuz after Pakistan’s defense minister said Washington and Tehran are “close to some sort of arrangement.”

【咖哩豬排指數顯示日元嚴重低估 建立人稱比大麥克指數更貼切】隨著當局干預匯市的影響正在消退，紐約梅隆銀行高階策略師Geoff Yu利用日本一道快餐主食的價格，以準確衡量日元的疲軟程度。Yu建立了咖哩豬排指數，作為《經濟學人》大麥克指數(Big Mac Index)的替代指標。他認為，相較於大麥克這種漢堡，比較咖哩豬排飯的國際價格，更能反映日元貶值對日本民眾究竟是什麼感受。週三上午的全球外匯市場上，1美元約兌159.23日元。但根據Yu的計算，在考量咖哩餐點價格反映的日元購買力後，1美元應該只能兌62.18日元。這意味著，外匯市場正大幅低估日元。相較之下，基於麥當勞漢堡各國價格的大麥克指數顯示，1美元應該可以兌換80.30日元。Yu說，如果目標是讓購買力向高收入國家看齊，這項指數顯示，日元需要更為強勁。他採用全球最大咖哩飯連鎖店CoCo壹番屋的價格計算，該公司在全球擁有約1500家分店。

### 2026-08-12 10:27:22  #Trading Economics #Markets #Indonesia #Stock Market #Importance 1

Indonesia Stocks Rebound — Indonesian shares rose 51 points or 0.8% to 6,319 in early deals on Wednesday, halting losses in the prior two sessions amid widespread gains across major sectors led by infrastructure, energy, basic materials, and cyclicals. sentiment improved following fresh data showing July car sales surged 33%, hinting at recovery in the automotive sector. Traders also took note of President Prabowo’s nomination of acting BI Governor Destry Damayanti as the sole candidate to succeed Perry Warjiyo, a move seen as providing policy continuity. Still, upside was tempered by overnight weakness on Wall Street and caution ahead of MSCI’s August 2026 Review later today, with rebalancing effective after August 31. Domestic concerns also lingered as retail sales fell for a third straight month in June, weighed by softness in discretionary categories. Standout movers included Indosat (8.4%), Petrindo Jaya Kreasi (5.6%), Bumi Resources (2.8%), and Japfa Comfeed (1.8%).

【Alibaba Cloud launches Lingjun Zhenwu M890 super-node instance; first batch on sale in Ulanqab】Alibaba Cloud has launched the Lingjun Zhenwu M890 super-node instance, with the first batch now on sale in the Ulanqab region. It is the first domestic super-node configuration reported to run models exceeding 2 trillion parameters; Kimi K3 and Qwen3.8 Max are available via the instance. Technical features include FP8/FP4 low-precision compute support and an ICN Switch 1.0 scale-up interconnect that raises node scale from 16 to 64 cards and boosts inter-card bandwidth to 800 GB/s. Alibaba Cloud reports training performance is roughly three times that of the previous-generation Zhenwu 810E in autonomous driving and embodied-intelligence training scenarios.

【India to tighten NGO oversight, may empower seizure of foreign-funded NGO assets】India plans to tighten regulation of NGOs, including a proposed amendment that would empower a new agency to seize land, buildings or other assets owned by foreign-funded organizations whose mandatory official registration is cancelled or not renewed. The ruling Modi-led Hindu nationalist government is prepared to push the reforms despite lobbying from Western governments, opposition parties and Christian churches warning the move could jeopardize thousands of schools, hospitals, religious sites and humanitarian projects. A senior Indian official said the government may seek a parliamentary vote on the amendment before this Thursday’s session ends. Officials cite concerns about covert foreign influence as the rationale for the tighter oversight.

【ANTHROPIC to embed invisible watermark in Claude outputs】ANTHROPIC said on Aug 11 it has signed the EU AI Act Article 50 guidelines on AI-generated content transparency. The marking will cover all major Claude products and access channels — Claude Platform (API), Claude, Claude Code, Claude Cowork and Claude Tag — and apply to cloud partners including AWS, Google Cloud and Microsoft Foundry. ANTHROPIC said the scheme will be implemented globally across all Claude product lines and partner cloud platforms, not limited to the EU.

Heating Oil Approaches 4-Month High — US heating oil futures advanced toward $4.30 per gallon on Wednesday, approaching a four-month high amid mounting concerns over tight distillate supplies. Yemen’s Houthis recently attacked Saudi Arabia’s Jazan refinery, which has been shut since July 27 following an earlier strike by the group. Plans to restart the facility have since been postponed from August 15 to August 30. Russia’s fuel export restrictions have also added to global supply concerns. This comes on top of uncertainty over the Strait of Hormuz, where markets are assessing the prospects for a potential US-Iran agreement on the key waterway. Pakistan’s defense minister said Washington and Tehran were “close to some sort of arrangement,” while a Qatari official cited by Al Jazeera said Iran-Oman talks had reached an advanced stage. Meanwhile, US refiners are processing crude at the highest seasonal pace since 2018, despite capacity falling by 600,000 barrels per day over the same period.

【最高法釋出7件特許經營典型案例 明確被特許人享有“冷靜期”單方解除權】今天，最高人民法院釋出7件關於特許經營的典型案例，引導和規範經營主體誠信合規經營，促進特許經營健康有序發展。其中一個特點是依法保障被特許人合法權益。在特許經營活動中，特許人與被特許人資訊不對稱、簽約能力不對等，被特許人處於相對弱勢地位，容易發生權利義務失衡。案例二中，雖然涉案合同未明確約定被特許人在特許經營合同訂立後一定期限內可以單方解除合同（俗稱“冷靜期”條款），但人民法院明確被特許人享有的“冷靜期”單方解除權屬於法定權利，被特許人可以在經營資源尚未被實際利用的合理期限內依法行使。案例五中，特許人在合同履行期間被撤銷備案資格，履約能力明顯弱化，人民法院依法支援了被特許人解除合同的訴請。(央視新聞)

Other notable gainers included SK Square (9.1%), Hyundai Motor (1.4%), LG Energy Solution (3.4%), Hanwha Aerospace (3.0%), KB Financial Group (1.0%), Kia Corporation (1.6%), and Doosan Enerbility (3.0%). Investors also looked ahead to the US July inflation report, with a softer-than-expected reading potentially easing concerns over a September Federal Reserve rate hike. Gasoline Rises to 2-Week High — US gasoline futures rose to around $3.16 per gallon on Wednesday, a two-week high, tracking gains in crude prices as markets assessed the viability of efforts toward a potential deal on the Strait of Hormuz. Pakistan’s defense minister said the US and Iran are “close to some sort of arrangement” over the waterway, while Iran-Oman talks have reached an advanced stage, according to a Qatari official cited by Al Jazeera. Meanwhile, API data showed gasoline stocks fell by 1.531 million barrels in the week ending August 7, following a 156,000-barrel increase the prior week. Elsewhere, escalating Russia-Ukraine attacks raised concerns over further energy disruptions after Ukraine launched a long-range drone strike on a major Russian refinery. Russia’s overseas crude shipments fell to their lowest since May, while Moscow extended its ban on gasoline and diesel exports through January 2027. At the pump, the EIA raised its retail gasoline price forecast for this year and 2027.

【Henan issues red heavy-rain warning, activates level-2 flood emergency】Henan’s flood-control command activated a level-2 emergency response at 08:00 on Aug 12, the provincial emergency management department said. From 08:00 Aug 12 to 08:00 Aug 13 heavy rain is expected along the Huai River and across much of northern Henan. Heavy downpours are forecast in parts of southern Jiaozuo, central-eastern and eastern Xinxiang, eastern Luoyang, Zhengzhou, Kaifeng, central-western Shangqiu, Pingdingshan, Xuchang, Luohe, central-western and northern Zhoukou, northeastern Nanyang and northern Zhumadian. Localised ultra-heavy rain (250–350mm at isolated points) is possible in southeastern Zhengzhou, southern Kaifeng, central Pingdingshan, northern Xuchang and western Zhoukou. Brief intense rainfall with hourly rates of 40–60mm (up to 80mm locally) and convective thunderstorm winds of force 8–10 are also expected.

European Gas Holds Losses as Traders Assess Hormuz Developments — European natural gas prices hovered around €60 per MWh on Wednesday after falling in the previous session, as traders assessed diplomatic efforts to reopen the Strait of Hormuz and resume LNG flows from the Gulf. Pakistan’s defense minister said the US and Iran were “close to some sort of arrangement” over the waterway, while negotiations between Tehran and Oman were also reportedly making significant progress. However, uncertainty over a swift reopening of the strait persists, with both sides taking firmer positions. The ongoing disruption to shipping has severely constrained LNG shipments, leaving shipments from major exporter Qatar significantly delayed. Analysts expect European gas prices to keep a firm floor until storage levels show clearer signs of building toward seasonal targets before winter. Hot weather across Europe is also boosting electricity demand for cooling, adding further pressure to the market.

### 2026-08-12 09:07:48  #MKT News #Market Themes #The Trump Trade #S&P500 #Impact bullish #Nasdaq100 #Dow

【Trump weighs inflation-indexed capital gains cut ahead of midterms; market implications】Trump is considering capital gains relief ahead of the midterm elections, including inflation-indexing of capital gains and raising the home-sale exclusion (currently $250,000 individual / $500,000 married). Indexing would tax only real gains — e.g., a 50% nominal gain with 20% inflation taxed only on the residual real appreciation — effectively lowering the tax burden on long-term holdings of equities, real estate and businesses and likely supporting asset prices and trading activity. Larry Kudlow publicly promoted the idea in July. These are policy signals, not law: direct rate changes or expanded exclusions require congressional legislation, and any administrative inflation-indexing would face substantial legal challenge.

### 2026-08-12 09:01:24  #MKT News

【China launches first double‑deck bulk rail‑sea intermodal half‑height container train】Today (12th) China launched its first double‑deck bulk rail‑sea intermodal half‑height container train from Baotou Wanshuiquan South station, Inner Mongolia, to Huanghua port, Hebei. The service links double‑deck rail transport with sea shipping without re‑boxing: standard 2.6m containers are compressed to 1.7m half‑height units compatible with existing port‑rail handling, enabling single‑box end‑to‑end transfer, cutting more than five transshipment steps and reducing unit transport cost by over 25%. The operator describes the option as a no‑rebox, no‑open, one‑box‑through logistics solution for bulk cargo.

Silver Steadies Ahead of US CPI Report — Silver steadied near $65 an ounce on Wednesday after declining in the previous session, as investors prepared for key US inflation data that could provide fresh clues on the Federal Reserve’s policy outlook. Markets remain divided over whether the Fed will raise rates by 25 basis points in September after keeping them unchanged in July, with rising oil prices reinforcing a hawkish bias. Meanwhile, traders assessed the prospects of a US-Iran deal to reopen the Strait of Hormuz amid shifting signals. Pakistan’s defense minister said Washington and Tehran are “close to some sort of arrangement” over the strait, while reports indicated that talks between Iran and Oman have reached an advanced stage. Elsewhere, silver remained supported by solid industrial consumption, particularly from expanding solar panel manufacturing and electricity grid development. Recent data showed Chinese imports of silver-bearing ores jumped 62.5% year-on-year in June to 219,000 tonnes.

Four representative vessels will carry out phased validation of navigation under normal and adverse conditions and a full-line aids-to-navigation efficacy check. The trials are intended to underpin the canal’s scheduled opening in Sept 2026. The Pinglu Canal, a backbone project of the Western Land-Sea New Corridor, is 134.2 km long and designed for 5,000-ton vessels; on completion it will establish a river-sea intermodal trunk linking southwest China to the sea via the Xijiang mainstem.

【Oil rally sparks renewed inflation fears, JGB yields rise】Rising oil prices have rekindled inflation worries and pushed Japanese government bond yields higher. The five-year JGB yield hit a record high and the two-year yield rose to its strongest level since May 1995, prompting markets to increase bets on further BOJ tightening; Tokyo traders' association data place the probability of a September BOJ rate hike at roughly two-thirds. Domestic yield moves have not produced clear yen support: strategists say external forces — higher U.S. Treasury yields, firmer oil and a strong dollar/yen — remain the dominant drivers. Higher JGB yields could eventually help the yen by narrowing the Japan-U.S. yield gap, but current external pressure outweighs that effect. Wednesday's U.S. CPI print is the key near-term catalyst for the dollar and global yields.

Australian Market Tracks Wall Street Losses — Australian equities retreated Wednesday, with the benchmark slipping 40 points or 0.5% to 9,207 in morning trade, erasing the prior day’s modest uptick after Wall Street’s overnight weakness and stalled Strait of Hormuz talks lifted oil prices ahead of key inflation data. Locally, the Reserve Bank kept the cash rate steady at 4.35% after three hikes this year, stressing inflation remains uncomfortably high and expectations elevated. Sectoral weakness was led by tech services, manufacturing, consumer services, and retail, though consumer durables and logistics offered partial support. The four major banks eased between 0.1% and 0.7%, while notable laggards included BHP Group (-0.5%), South32 (-2.7%), Computershare (-2.0%), and Bluescope Steel (-1.8%). Market focus now shifts to testimony from Governor Michele Bullock before parliament on Friday, preceded by Assistant Governor Chris Kent’s appearance Thursday, as investors weigh policy signals against persistent inflationary pressures.

【South Korea to inject more than $700m into sovereign fund targeting AI, strategic industries next year】South Korea’s finance ministry expects to deploy 600 billion–1 trillion won (about $707m) of new capital into a sovereign-wealth fund targeting AI and other strategic industries next year, and the final amount could exceed initial estimates depending on selected targets and their funding needs, the ministry’s Innovation Growth Office head said on Tuesday. There are currently no plans for the fund to invest directly in Samsung Electronics or SK Hynix. In July Seoul said the Korea Investment Corporation would set up a “strategic industry investment account” with initial capital of at least 20 trillion won (about RMB94bn), targeting AI, semiconductors, data centres and overseas supply-chain core firms and due to be formally launched and operated next year. The fund will also target robotics, energy and battery and grid infrastructure; nuclear, space and quantum technologies are under consideration.

【中國銀河證券：FDE重構AI應用新正規化，軟體行業或迎價值重估】中國銀河證券研報稱，前沿部署工程師（Forward Deployed Engineer，簡稱“FDE”）將成為AI落地的標準化路徑，軟體行業或迎價值重估。AI大模型時代，FDE不再是傳統意義上的人力外包的淺層定位，而是依託於前向程式碼能力與業務know-how，將通用大模型能力深度落地並沉澱為企業可複用的資料資產與智慧基座，所以傳統軟體行業收入增長將不再依靠傳統軟體授權模式，AI定製化服務、私有化部署、模型運維、駐場工程服務等將提高客單價。中國銀河證券認為，FDE帶來軟體行業交付正規化革命，提升客戶黏性以及客單價，商業模式上從傳統軟體授權轉向ROI、使用效果導向的全生命週期增量價值服務，故行業know-how將成為未來AI應用公司的護城河，部分軟體公司將迎來價值體系重估，開啟估值天花板。

【美國當前財報季出現反常態勢 科技巨頭取代小盤股成波動主力】期權分析服務商ORATS的資料顯示，本財報季中，人工智慧(AI)熱潮推動的超大規模雲服務商股價在財報釋出後出現了比往常更大的波動，這打破了以往小型公司財報通常引發更大幅度股價波動的歷史規律。財報季的前幾周通常由市場龍頭企業主導，包括摩根大通和富國銀行等金融巨頭、率先發布財報的道瓊斯藍籌股，以及蘋果、微軟、Alphabet和Meta等超大市值科技巨頭。指數中的中小型公司則在後幾周成為市場焦點。但這次情況有所不同。“最近幾周，小型公司財報引發的波動較為平淡——與前幾周形成鮮明對比，當時超大規模雲服務商的財報引發了比往常更大的股價波動，”ORATS創始人Matt Amberson表示。包括亞馬遜、微軟、谷歌和Meta在內的一些超大規模科技公司，在財報釋出後股價出現大幅波動，超過了各自過去幾個季度的平均波動幅度，因為投資者基於AI資本支出是否產生回報的跡象，而積極買入或拋售股票。

【中信證券：AI重塑遊戲行業 重點推薦具備AI UGC平臺性機遇的公司】中信證券研報表示，2025年以來，生成式AI持續降低遊戲研發門檻，推動內容生產從“人力密集”走向“智慧增強”，行業供給側由此迎來顯著擴容。但市場並未因此線性進入“百花齊放”的價值兌現階段，相反，玩家注意力、產品差異化與長線運營能力變得更加稀缺。中信證券認為，AI不會簡單攤薄遊戲行業的價值，反而將推動行業價值錨點從“能否生產內容”轉向“能否持續創造精品、獲取流量並完成商業化兌現”。遊戲行業正在從“產能約束”時代，走向“稀缺性重估”時代，其中頭部公司最為受益。中信證券判斷，AI在遊戲產業鏈的價值釋放主要分為兩個階段：短期，AI進入頭部廠商研發管線，提升長青產品維護、高規格新品開發和全球化試錯能力；中長期，隨著模型能力提升、Token成本下降和平臺基礎設施完善，AI有望透過AI UGC擴大內容供給、透過AI Native創造新型體驗，進一步擴大市場規模。重點推薦全鏈路工業化能力領先的頭部公司，具備AI UGC平臺性機遇的公司，並建議關注具備內容平臺基礎的企業。

Gold Steadies Ahead of US CPI Report — Gold steadied around $4,370 an ounce on Wednesday following a volatile start to the week, as investors prepared for key US inflation data that could provide fresh clues on the Federal Reserve’s policy outlook. Meanwhile, traders assessed the prospects of a US-Iran deal to reopen the Strait of Hormuz amid shifting signals. Pakistan’s defense minister said Washington and Tehran are “close to some sort of arrangement” over the strait, while reports indicated that talks between Iran and Oman have reached an advanced stage. Elsewhere, gold continued to draw support from investment demand and central bank purchases, particularly from China. 【CITIC Securities: Rare-earth prices likely to remain steady-to-firm near term】CITIC Securities research note, citing CUSTOMS data: July rare-earth exports 4,223.5 t, down 29.54% YoY and 17.26% MoM, the lowest monthly level since March; Jan–Jul cumulative exports 34,706.3 t, down 10% YoY. ### 2026-08-12 08:05:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Singapore #Current Account #Importance 1

Singapore Current Account Surplus Widens in Q2 — Singapore’s current account surplus increased to SGD 37.08 billion in the second quarter of 2026, up from SGD 30.52 billion in the same period of 2025. The increase came as the goods account surplus surged to SGD 70.39 billion from SGD 60.12 billion a year earlier amid a surge in exports. Also, the services account surplus advanced to SGD 14.33 billion from SGD 12.27 billion. Additionally, the secondary income deficit narrowed to SGD 2.88 billion from SGD 4.00 billion. Meanwhile, the primary income deficit climbed to SGD 44.77 billion from SGD 37.87 billion over the same period. Last year, the country recorded a current account surplus of SGD 131.96 billion, widening slightly from SGD 131.67 billion in 2024.

【China International Capital Co recommends continued overweight in gold】China International Capital Co says the two narratives that had pressured gold are unraveling: global liquidity is not in a sustained tightening cycle and de-dollarization remains active. US disinflation and slower growth support a move toward easier policy, while Fed rhetoric looks hawkish but policy actions are effectively dovish; Fed reform could open space for future rate cuts. Dollar balance-sheet reduction would help restore dollar credibility in principle but faces heavy market and political constraints, and structural erosion of dollar credit from high debt, deficits and policy uncertainty is hard to reverse. Global central bank net gold purchases rebounded to 289 tonnes in Q2, up 62% YoY and a Q2 record, signalling entrenched dollar concerns and continued reserve-diversification demand for gold. With looser global liquidity reducing upward pressure on real rates and the dollar, gold should regain support from both liquidity and reserve diversification. The firm judges the gold bull market is intact, the reallocation window after the recent correction is open, and recommends maintaining an overweight in gold.

【調查階段預警退市風險 監管質效進一步提升】近日，上海證監局、遼寧證監局相繼公佈卓然股份、萃華珠寶兩起案件的階段性調查情況，向市場提示兩家公司存在觸及重大違法強制退市情形的風險。調查環節預警企業退市風險，在資本市場尚屬首次，折射出資本市場監管質效的進一步升級。按照慣例，監管部門一般在行政處罰事先告知階段，才會明確涉事公司涉嫌觸及重大違法強制退市情形，在此之前的案件調查期間，非必要情況，監管部門不會主動披露案件階段性調查情況。從立案調查到行政處罰事先告知書下發，大多需要經歷數月甚至更長時間的調查取證。然而，上述兩起個案退市預警的釋出時間，均是在調查尚未終結、行政處罰事先告知書尚未作出的階段，時間節點明顯前移。業內人士指出，監管部門及時公開階段性調查結果，尤其是涉嫌財務造假類案件的調查情況，能夠更早地向市場釋放退市風險訊號，便利投資者及時調整投資策略，壓縮市場炒作空間，避免不實資訊對市場的干擾，促使市場定價迴歸基本面。

【瑞士再保險瑞再研究院：野火威脅加劇令保險風險居高不下】瑞士再保險(Swiss Re)的研究部門表示，2026年上半年保險業的自然災害估算損失大幅下降，但野火和天氣事件帶來的風險依然很高。瑞士再保險瑞再研究院週二警告稱，野火風險是全球增長最快的氣象災害。該機構還表示，6月份創紀錄的高溫和乾燥天氣增加了歐洲迎來活躍野火季的可能性。瑞士再保險的巨災風險主管巴爾茨.格羅利蒙德(Balz Grollimund)表示：“歐洲最近的野火凸顯出，更炎熱、更乾燥的天氣正導致大型野火更有可能發生，而且隨著越來越多的住宅、商業和基礎設施建在風險敞口較大的地區，災害造成的損失也更為慘重。”瑞士再保險瑞再研究院的資料顯示，2026年上半年，自然災害造成的保險損失估算為420億美元，低於上年同期的910億美元。該機構表示，這一數字創下了自2020年新冠疫情高峰期以來的上半年最低水平，也比500億美元的10年平均水平低16%。該機構表示，上半年自然災害損失較少並不一定意味著全年都會比較平靜，因為受北大西洋颶風季的影響，保險損失通常更多地集中在下半年。

### 2026-08-12 07:29:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #Russia #Balance of Trade #Importance 0

Russia Trade Surplus Widens in June — The Russian trade surplus widened to USD 12.5 billion in June of 2026 from USD 8.2 billion in the corresponding period of the previous year. Exports climbed by 32.6% year-on-year to USD 43.3 billion, the highest this year, supported by stronger energy revenues as oil and gas prices remained elevated amid disruptions to tanker flows from the Persian Gulf following the escalation of the war in the Middle East. Higher global energy prices boosted the value of Russian crude and gas shipments, while the easing of some US sanctions also supported exports. Meanwhile, imports rose by 26% to USD 30.8 billion, reflecting stronger purchases of machinery, equipment and transport vehicles as domestic demand and investment remained resilient.

【Chip boom lifts Japan manufacturing confidence to highest since March】REUTERS TANKAN shows Japan manufacturing confidence rose from 13 in July to 18 in August, the strongest reading since March 2026. Non-manufacturing confidence gained to 28 from 25, supported by robust domestic consumption. The three‑month outlook signals a moderation in sentiment. Semiconductor‑related demand is the main driver: sharp jumps in chemical and metal‑machinery subindices suggest strength in the semiconductor supply chain is spreading across Japan’s industrial base rather than remaining concentrated in a few chipmakers. The manufacturing index peak implies earlier global trade uncertainty that had weighed on chip‑demand exporters has largely faded. The transportation equipment index remained at 0, indicating the auto sector has not yet meaningfully benefited from the broader recovery.

Brent Holds Advance as US-Iran Deal Eyed — Brent held above $89 per barrel on Wednesday after rising for five consecutive sessions, as investors assessed mixed signals surrounding a potential deal between the US and Iran. 【宏觀利好疊加工業需求加持 銀價短期有望偏強震盪】近期，國際貴金屬市場熱度升溫。倫敦現貨黃金價格從3940美元/盎司附近一路衝高至4400美元/盎司上方，倫敦現貨白銀價格也由54美元/盎司附近最高升破66美元/盎司，兩者區間累計最大漲幅分別超12%、21%。本輪上漲行情中，白銀市場呈現行情啟動滯後、價格漲勢更猛的特徵。業內人士認為，美國非農就業資料等表現不及預期、美債收益率下行、美元指數回落以及市場對美聯儲貨幣政策預期調整，共同改善了近期貴金屬價格執行的宏觀環境。全球央行持續購金也為貴金屬板塊提供了中長期支撐，而光伏、新能源等領域的工業需求又賦予白銀區別於黃金的基本面支撐。另外，白銀市場規模相對較小、價格彈性更高，一旦資金風險偏好改善，其上漲幅度往往更加突出。短期來看，銀價依託多重利好支撐，有望延續偏強震盪態勢。銀價中長期走勢則高度依賴美聯儲貨幣政策轉向節奏等因素，行情整體易漲難跌。（中國證券報）
